def resolve_token_ids_exact(tokenizer, target: str,
                            candidates: Tuple[str, ...]) -> List[int]:
    """MI-2-P4-Auflösung (Oberflächen-Exakt, gebucht 2026-10-26):
    len(encoded) == 1 UND decode([id]) == target EXAKT (kein lstrip);
    Mehr-Token-Kandidaten abgewiesen, bevor ein Vertreter entsteht."""
    ids: List[int] = []
    for cand in candidates:
        encoded = tokenizer.encode(cand, add_special_tokens=False)
        if len(encoded) != 1:
            continue
        decoded_first = tokenizer.decode([encoded[0]], skip_special_tokens=True)
        if decoded_first == target:
            ids.append(int(encoded[0]))
    return ids


# -- CPU-Pilot (PILOT-Labels, promovieren keine Verdicts — R2) ---------------
def pilot_rows() -> List[Tuple[str, str, str]]:
    import torch as _torch

    rows: List[Tuple[str, str, str]] = []

    # pilot_band_math: SET-Semantik des echten Helfers an Stub-Buffer.
    buf = _torch.tensor([1.5, 2.5, 3.5, 4.5, 5.5, 6.5, 7.5, 8.5])
    apply_band_steer(buf, (1, 3), 50.0)
    ok_band = _torch.equal(buf, _torch.tensor(
        [-50.0, 50.0, -50.0, 50.0, -50.0, -50.0, -50.0, -50.0]))

    # pilot_knockout_math: ADDITIVE Semantik des echten Helfers.
    buf2 = _torch.tensor([1.5, 2.5, 3.5, 4.5, 5.5, 6.5, 7.5, 8.5])
    apply_expert_knockout(buf2, (0, 2), -50.0)
    ok_knock = _torch.equal(buf2, _torch.tensor(
        [-48.5, 2.5, -46.5, 4.5, 5.5, 6.5, 7.5, 8.5]))

    # pilot_restore_math: C3-Runde (clone -> mutate -> copy_ -> equal).
    orig = buf.clone()
    apply_expert_knockout(buf, (5,), -50.0)
    buf.copy_(orig)
    ok_restore = _torch.equal(buf, orig)
    ok_restore = ok_restore and not _torch.equal(buf, orig * 0 + 1.0)

    # pilot_tv_math: canon_tv-Identität/Differenz.
    a = _torch.softmax(_torch.zeros(8), dim=0)
    b = _torch.softmax(_torch.tensor([3.0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]), dim=0)
    ok_tv = canon_tv(a, a) == 0.0 and canon_tv(a, b) > 0.0

    # pilot_select_math: Bänder + Experten-Sets konsistent.
    ok_select = (
        len(BAND_EDGE) == 6 and len(BAND_CORE) == 20
        and BAND_GLOBAL == (5, 11, 17, 23)
        and all(0 <= e < 8 for e in EX_WITT)
        and all(0 <= e < 8 for e in KO_PROSE + KO_CODE)
        and set(KO_ANOM_A + KO_ANOM_B) <= set(range(8)))

    rows.append(("pilot_band_math", "PILOT" if ok_band else "BROKEN",
                 f"band-template: {buf.tolist() if not ok_band else 'ok'}"))
    rows.append(("pilot_knockout_math", "PILOT" if ok_knock else "BROKEN",
                 "additiv: ok" if ok_knock else f"knockout: {buf2.tolist()}"))
    rows.append(("pilot_restore_math", "PILOT" if ok_restore else "BROKEN",
                 f"C3-Runde: {'bitidentisch' if ok_restore else 'Mismatch'}"))
    rows.append(("pilot_tv_math", "PILOT" if ok_tv else "BROKEN",
                 f"tv(a,a)=0, tv(a,b)>0: {ok_tv}"))
    rows.append(("pilot_select_math", "PILOT" if ok_select else "BROKEN",
                 "Baender+Sets registriert ok" if ok_select else "Fehler"))
    return rows


# -- GPU-Lane -----------------------------------------------------------------
def eval_purity(probe: MechInterpProbe, oracle: Any, fp_before: Dict[str, Any],
                p_before: torch.Tensor, results: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """V1 — Serien-Interventionen, NACH der Serie fingerprint + Kontroll-TV."""
    # Serien-Bedingungen (P2 registriert): jede in eigenem Block.
    with probe.steer_band(EX_WITT, BAND_EDGE):
        pass
    with probe.steer_band(EX_WITT, BAND_CORE):
        pass
    with probe.steer_band(EX_WITT, BAND_GLOBAL):
        pass
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_PROSE, BAND_CORE):
        pass
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_CODE, BAND_CORE):
        pass
    fp_after = probe.fingerprint()
    equal = probe.fingerprint_equal(fp_before, fp_after)
    p_after = probe.last_probs(GENERIC_PROMPT)
    tv = canon_tv(p_before, p_after)
    results["purity"] = {
        "fingerprint_equal": bool(equal),
        "control_tv": tv,
        "tv_zero": bool(tv == 0.0),
        "series": ["steer_band(EX_WITT, EDGE)", "steer_band(EX_WITT, CORE)",
                   "steer_band(EX_WITT, GLOBAL)",
                   "knockout(KO_PROSE, CORE)", "knockout(KO_CODE, CORE)"],
    }


def eval_sufficiency(probe: MechInterpProbe, oracle: Any,
                     results: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """V2 — SP3-Stummkanal am GENERIC_PROMPT; Steerings als dict-Direkt-
    steuerung (SET, {1,3}) über die Bänder (oracle.steer dict-Arm-Canon)."""
    m_raw = probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)
    with oracle.steer({"experts": list(EX_WITT), "steer": STEER_AMPLITUDE}):
        m_full = probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)
    with probe.steer_band(EX_WITT, BAND_CORE):
        m_core = probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)
    with probe.steer_band(EX_WITT, BAND_EDGE):
        m_edge = probe.digit_mass(GENERIC_PROMPT)
    dm_full = m_full["M_digit"] - m_raw["M_digit"]
    dm_core = m_core["M_digit"] - m_raw["M_digit"]
    dm_edge = m_edge["M_digit"] - m_raw["M_digit"]
    results["sufficiency"] = {
        "m_raw": m_raw, "m_full": m_full, "m_core": m_core, "m_edge": m_edge,
        "dm_full": dm_full, "dm_core": dm_core, "dm_edge": dm_edge,
        "premise_raw_dumb": bool(m_raw["unknown"]),
        "full_opens": bool(dm_full > 0.0),
        "core_ratio": (dm_core / dm_full) if dm_full > 0.0 else None,
        "core_sufficient": bool(dm_full > 0.0
                                and dm_core >= SUFF_RATIO_CORE * dm_full),
        "edge_smaller": bool(dm_core > dm_edge),
    }


def eval_domain_knockout(probe: MechInterpProbe, results: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """V3 — Double Dissociation im Core-Band; TVs per Text (EINHEIT T1/R3)."""
    texts = DOMAINS["prose"] + DOMAINS["code"] + DOMAINS["math_logic"]
    labels = (["prose"] * 3) + (["code"] * 3) + (["math_logic"] * 3)
    raw_p = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    tv_prose_ko: Dict[str, List[float]] = {"ko_prose": [], "ko_code": [],
                                           "ko_anom_a": [], "ko_anom_b": []}
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_PROSE, BAND_CORE) as _:
        ko_p1 = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_CODE, BAND_CORE):
        ko_p2 = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_ANOM_A, (11,)):
        ko_p3 = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_ANOM_B, (11,)):
        ko_p4 = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
    for i, (t, label, p0, p1, p2, p3, p4) in enumerate(
            zip(texts, labels, raw_p, ko_p1, ko_p2, ko_p3, ko_p4)):
        tv1, tv2, tv3, tv4 = (canon_tv(p0, p1), canon_tv(p0, p2),
                              canon_tv(p0, p3), canon_tv(p0, p4))
        rows.append({"text": t, "domain": label, "tv_ko_prose": tv1,
                     "tv_ko_code": tv2, "tv_ko_anom_a": tv3,
                     "tv_ko_anom_b": tv4})
        print(f"  [row {i}] {label}: ko_prose={tv1:.4f} ko_code={tv2:.4f}"
              f" anomal_k47={tv3:.4f} kontrolle_k12={tv4:.4f}")
        if label == "prose":
            tv_prose_ko["ko_prose"].append(tv1)
            tv_prose_ko["ko_code"].append(tv2)
            tv_prose_ko["ko_anom_a"].append(tv3)
            tv_prose_ko["ko_anom_b"].append(tv4)

    def _mean(xs: List[float]) -> float:
        return float(sum(xs) / len(xs)) if xs else 0.0

    prose_kopro = _mean(tv_prose_ko["ko_prose"])
    prose_kocode = _mean(tv_prose_ko["ko_code"])
    code_kopro = _mean([r["tv_ko_prose"] for r in rows if r["domain"] == "code"])
    code_kocode = _mean([r["tv_ko_code"] for r in rows if r["domain"] == "code"])
    results["domain_knockout"] = {
        "rows_9": rows,   # 9 Rows (3x prose/code/math_logic)
        "prose_mean_ko_prose": prose_kopro,
        "prose_mean_ko_code": prose_kocode,
        "code_mean_ko_prose": code_kopro,
        "code_mean_ko_code": code_kocode,
        "dissociation_prose": bool(prose_kopro > prose_kocode),
        "dissociation_code": bool(code_kocode > code_kopro),
    }


def eval_paris_resilience(probe: MechInterpProbe, oracle: Any,
                          paris_id: int, results: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """V4 — top1 == 9079 unter 5 registrierten Bedingungen."""
    conditions: Dict[str, int] = {}
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_PROSE, BAND_CORE):
        conditions["ko_prose@core"] = int(probe.last_probs(PARIS_TEXT).argmax().item())
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_CODE, BAND_CORE):
        conditions["ko_code@core"] = int(probe.last_probs(PARIS_TEXT).argmax().item())
    for preset in PRESETS:
        with oracle.steer(preset):
            conditions[f"preset_{preset}"] = int(
                probe.last_probs(PARIS_TEXT).argmax().item())
    stable = all(v == paris_id for v in conditions.values())
    results["paris_resilience"] = {
        "paris_id": paris_id,
        "conditions_top1": conditions,
        "stable": bool(stable),
    }


def eval_l17_localization(probe: MechInterpProbe, paris_id: int,
                          results: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """V5 — (A) Sublayer-Datum auf 9077 (P4-Referenz); (B) Einzel-Experten-
    KOs in ℓ17 vs ℓ10 (16 Forwards, per-Experte persistiert)."""
    sub = probe.sublayer_logits(PARIS_TEXT, {"id": paris_id, "str": " Paris"})
    r17 = sub["rows"][17]
    da, dm = r17["dp_attn"], r17["dp_moe"]
    share = da / (da + dm) if (da + dm) != 0.0 else 0.0

    def _sum_abs_dp(layer: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        deltas: Dict[str, float] = {}
        raw = probe.last_probs(PARIS_TEXT)[paris_id].item()
        for e in range(8):
            with probe.knockout_experts((e,), (layer,)):
                ko = probe.last_probs(PARIS_TEXT)[paris_id].item()
            deltas[str(e)] = abs(ko - raw)
        return {"total": float(sum(deltas.values())), "per_expert": deltas,
                "p_raw": raw}

    s17 = _sum_abs_dp(17)
    s10 = _sum_abs_dp(10)
    results["l17_localization"] = {
        "sublayer_17": {"dp_attn": da, "dp_moe": dm, "attn_share": share,
                        "anchor_tv": sub["anchor_tv"],
                        "telescope_broken": sub["telescope_broken"]},
        "ko_total_17": s17, "ko_total_10": s10,
        "l17_lion": bool(share > 0.5),
        "l17_stronger": bool(s17["total"] > s10["total"]),
    }


def eval_l11_anomaly(probe: MechInterpProbe, results: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """V6 — KO {4,7} vs {1,2} in GENAU ℓ11 auf den 3 prose-Texten."""
    texts = DOMAINS["prose"]
    raw_p = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_ANOM_A, (11,)):
        ko_a = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    with probe.knockout_experts(KO_ANOM_B, (11,)):
        ko_b = [probe.last_probs(t) for t in texts]
    tv_a = [canon_tv(p, q) for p, q in zip(raw_p, ko_a)]
    tv_b = [canon_tv(p, q) for p, q in zip(raw_p, ko_b)]
    mean_a = float(sum(tv_a) / len(tv_a))
    mean_b = float(sum(tv_b) / len(tv_b))
    results["l11_anomaly"] = {
        "tv_k47": tv_a, "tv_k12": tv_b,
        "mean_k47": mean_a, "mean_k12": mean_b,
        "outlier_causal": bool(mean_a > mean_b),
    }


def run_gpu(checkpoint_base: str, checkpoint_machines: str,
            results: Dict[str, Any], findings: List[Tuple[str, str, str]]) -> None:
    from machines.verify_machines import _load_machines_model

    model, tokenizer = _load_machines_model(checkpoint_base, checkpoint_machines)
    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = False
    torch.use_deterministic_algorithms(True)
    device = torch.device("cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu")
    probe = MechInterpProbe(model, tokenizer, device=device,
                            max_length=MAX_LENGTH)
    from machines.machine_base import ArgonOracle
    oracle = ArgonOracle(model, tokenizer, device=device)

    # Auflösung (Oberflächen-Exakt, keine Vertreter):
    paris_ids = resolve_token_ids_exact(tokenizer, " Paris", (" Paris",))
    results["resolve"] = {"paris": paris_ids}
    if len(paris_ids) != 1:
        findings.append((
            "mi3_resolve_target",
            "OFFEN",
            f"Paris-Auflösung nicht eindeutig ({paris_ids}) — OFFEN ist "
            "kein PASS; sonstiges gemessen und persistent (keine Band-"
            "Erfindung)."))
        paris_id = paris_ids[0] if paris_ids else -1
    else:
        paris_id = paris_ids[0]

    fp_before = probe.fingerprint()
    p_before = probe.last_probs(GENERIC_PROMPT)

    for name, fn in (
            ("mi3_intervention_purity",
             lambda: eval_purity(probe, oracle, fp_before, p_before, results)),
            ("mi3_core_vs_edge_sufficiency",
             lambda: eval_sufficiency(probe, oracle, results)),
            ("mi3_domain_knockout_specificity",
             lambda: eval_domain_knockout(probe, results)),
            ("mi3_paris_circuit_resilience",
             lambda: eval_paris_resilience(probe, oracle, paris_id, results)),
            ("mi3_layer17_paris_localization",
             lambda: eval_l17_localization(probe, paris_id, results)),
            ("mi3_prose_l11_anomaly_probe",
             lambda: eval_l11_anomaly(probe, results)),
    ):
        try:
            fn()
        except Exception as exc:      # noqa: BLE001 — BROKEN-Buchung
            results.setdefault("broken", {})[name] = f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"
            findings.append((
                name, "BROKEN",
                f"GPU-Lane brach: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc} "
                "(kein stiller Fallback; sonstiges gemessen bleibt)."))


# -- classification + main ----------------------------------------------------
# Registrierter Verdict-Name -> Ergebnis-Schluessel der evals
# (KORREKTUR-LOG Lauf 1: Klassifikation pruefte sonst Mitgliedschaft
# unter Lang-Namen, waehrend die evals unter Kurz-Schluesseln buchen).
RESULT_KEYS = {
    "mi3_intervention_purity": "purity",
    "mi3_core_vs_edge_sufficiency": "sufficiency",
    "mi3_domain_knockout_specificity": "domain_knockout",
    "mi3_paris_circuit_resilience": "paris_resilience",
    "mi3_layer17_paris_localization": "l17_localization",
    "mi3_prose_l11_anomaly_probe": "l11_anomaly",
}


def classify(name: str, results: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, str]:
    """P4-Einstufung je registrierten Bänder (kein Re-Decide, kein
    Band-Kompromiss)."""
    if name == "mi3_intervention_purity":
        p = results["purity"]
        return ("PASS", f"fingerprint={p['fingerprint_equal']} "
                        f"control_tv={p['control_tv']!r}") if (
            p["fingerprint_equal"] and p["tv_zero"]) else (
            "FAIL", f"fingerprint={p['fingerprint_equal']} "
                        f"control_tv={p['control_tv']!r}")

    if name == "mi3_core_vs_edge_sufficiency":
        p = results["sufficiency"]
        if p["premise_raw_dumb"] is False:
            return ("OFFEN",
                    f"Premise nicht repliziert: M_raw={p['m_raw']['M_digit']:.4f} "
                    f"(≥ tau={M_DIGIT_TAU}) — volle Daten persistent.")
        if not p["full_opens"]:
            return ("OFFEN",
                    f"ΔM_full={p['dm_full']:.4f} ≤ 0 — Steering öffnet den "
                    "Digit-Kanal hier nicht (keine Band-Erfindung); Daten "
                    "persistent.")
        if p["core_sufficient"] and p["edge_smaller"]:
            return ("PASS",
                    f"ΔM_full={p['dm_full']:.4f} core_ratio="
                    f"{p['core_ratio']:.3f} edge={p['dm_edge']:.4f}")
        return ("FAIL",
                f"ΔM_full={p['dm_full']:.4f} core_ratio="
                f"{p['core_ratio']:.3f} core_sufficient="
                f"{p['core_sufficient']} edge_smaller={p['edge_smaller']}")

    if name == "mi3_domain_knockout_specificity":
        p = results["domain_knockout"]
        ok = p["dissociation_prose"] and p["dissociation_code"]
        note = (f"prose: ko_prose={p['prose_mean_ko_prose']:.4f} > "
                f"ko_code={p['prose_mean_ko_code']:.4f}; "
                f"code: ko_code={p['code_mean_ko_code']:.4f} > "
                f"ko_prose={p['code_mean_ko_prose']:.4f}")
        return ("PASS", note) if ok else ("FAIL", note)

    if name == "mi3_paris_circuit_resilience":
        p = results["paris_resilience"]
        note = ("top1-Bedingungen: "
                + ", ".join(f"{k}={v}" for k, v in p["conditions_top1"].items())
                + f" (paris_id={p['paris_id']})")
        return ("PASS", note) if p["stable"] else ("FAIL", note)

    if name == "mi3_layer17_paris_localization":
        p = results["l17_localization"]
        note = (f"attn_share(17)={p['sublayer_17']['attn_share']:.3f} "
                f"Σ|Δp|(17)={p['ko_total_17']['total']:.4f} vs "
                f"Σ|Δp|(10)={p['ko_total_10']['total']:.4f}")
        if p["l17_lion"] and p["l17_stronger"]:
            return ("PASS", note)
        return ("FAIL", note)

    if name == "mi3_prose_l11_anomaly_probe":
        p = results["l11_anomaly"]
        note = (f"Mittel ko{{4,7}}(ℓ11)={p['mean_k47']:.4f} vs "
                f"ko{{1,2}}(ℓ11)={p['mean_k12']:.4f}")
        return ("PASS", note) if p["outlier_causal"] else ("FAIL", note)

    return ("OFFEN", "unregistriertes Verdict — kein stiller Fallback.")


def main() -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="MI-3: Kausale Interventionen (steer_band + knockout)")
    ap.add_argument("--gpu", action="store_true")
    ap.add_argument("--checkpoint_base", default="./argon_1b")
    ap.add_argument("--checkpoint_machines", default="./argon_1b_machines")
    ap.add_argument("--json", default=None,
                    help="Ergebnis-Pfad; Default: GPU->MI-3-Datei, "
                         "CPU-Lane schreibt OHNE --json nichts "
                         "(P3-Schutz, MI-2-P4-Muster)")
    args = ap.parse_args()

    t0 = time.time()
    findings: List[Tuple[str, str, str]] = list(pilot_rows())

    data: Dict[str, Any] = {"pilot": [
        {"name": n, "status": s, "note": note} for n, s, note in findings]}

    gpu_broken = False
    if args.gpu:
        results: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        try:
            run_gpu(args.checkpoint_base, args.checkpoint_machines,
                    results, findings)
        except Exception as exc:      # noqa: BLE001 — BROKEN-Buchung
            gpu_broken = True
            findings.append((
                "gpu_broken", "BROKEN",
                f"GPU-Lane brach vor der Messung: {type(exc).__name__}: "
                f"{exc} (kein stiller Fallback)."))
        else:
            data["gpu"] = results

    verdicts = [f for f in findings if not f[0].startswith("pilot_")]
    classified: List[Tuple[str, str, str]] = []
    registered = ("mi3_intervention_purity", "mi3_core_vs_edge_sufficiency",
                  "mi3_domain_knockout_specificity",
                  "mi3_paris_circuit_resilience",
                  "mi3_layer17_paris_localization",
                  "mi3_prose_l11_anomaly_probe")
    if args.gpu and not gpu_broken:
        for name in registered:
            if name in {f[0] for f in findings if f[1] == "BROKEN"}:
                note = next(f[2] for f in findings
                            if f[0] == name and f[1] == "BROKEN")
                classified.append((name, "BROKEN", note))
            elif RESULT_KEYS[name] not in results:
                classified.append((
                    name, "OFFEN",
                    "GPU-Lane lieferte kein Datum — OFFEN ist kein PASS"))
            else:
                classified.append((name, *classify(name, results)))
    else:
        for name in registered:
            classified.append((
                name, "OFFEN",
                "GPU-Lane nicht gefahren oder Bruch gebucht (OFFEN ist "
                "kein PASS)."))
    findings.extend(classified)

    has_fail = any(s == "FAIL" for _, s, _ in classified)
    all_pass = all(s == "PASS" for _, s, _ in classified)

    for n, s, note in findings:
        print(f"[{s:6s}] {n}: {note}")
    print("-" * 88)
    counts = [sum(1 for _, s, _ in classified if s == k) for k in
              ("PASS", "OFFEN", "FAIL")]
    print(f"Verdikte: {counts[0]} PASS | {counts[1]} OFFEN | {counts[2]} FAIL"
          f"  ({time.time() - t0:.1f} s)")
    if has_fail:
        print("ROT — Falsifikator ausgelöst; keine Kürzung, volle Daten "
              "persistent.")
    elif all_pass and not gpu_broken:
        print("GRÜN — alle 6 Verdikte PASS; die Kausalität ist gemessen.")
    else:
        print("GELB — offen (GPU-Lane nicht gefahren oder Bruch gebucht; "
              "OFFEN ist kein PASS).")

    payload = {
        "all_pass": bool(all_pass and not has_fail and not gpu_broken),
        "open": [f[0] for f in classified if f[1] == "OFFEN"],
        "findings": [{"name": f[0], "status": f[1], "note": f[2]}
                     for f in findings],
        "data": data,
    }
    json_path = args.json if args.json is not None else \
        ("scratches/mi3_interventions.json" if args.gpu else None)
    if json_path is not None:
        with open(json_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
            json.dump(payload, handle, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        print(f"JSON: {json_path}")
    else:
        print("JSON: — (CPU-Lane schreibt ohne --json nichts)")
    if has_fail:
        return 1
    return 0 if (all_pass and not gpu_broken) else 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import sys as _sys
    raise SystemExit(main())

"""
machines/mech_interp.py
=======================
MechInterpProbe — Sonden-API für die mechanistische Interpretierbarkeit
der Argon-Maschinen (Spur B, MI-1..MI-4). Rein lesend; Interventionen
(Steering/Ablation) kommen in MI-3 über die Preset-Mechanik von
machine_base hinzu.

Canon-Anker (voll gelesen 2026-10-26):
 - moe_layers.py:56-93   ArgonEntropyAdaptiveRouter.forward
                         -> (top_indices, top_weights, router_entropy);
                         Top-K über scores + expert_bias (k = num_experts_per_tok);
                         last_counts nur im Training belegt — im Eval
                         verlassen, Capture läuft über Forward-Hooks.
 - moe_layers.py:128     gate-Aufruf innerhalb ArgonMoELayer.forward.
 - modeling_argon.py:601-611  Schicht-Schleife: (h, layer_entropy, _) = layer(...).
 - modeling_argon.py:615 h = self.norm(h)   (Final-Norm, Hook-Punkt).
 - modeling_argon.py:733 logits = self.lm_head(hidden_states)
 - modeling_argon.py:735-736 final_softcap — Checkpoint argon_1b_machines:
                         final_logit_softcapping = None  -> Lens-Pfad exakt
                         softmax(lm_head(norm(h))).
 - modeling_argon.py:188-190  ArgonHybridAttention: pattern=6,
                         is_global = (layer_idx % 6 == 5) -> globale
                         MLA-Schichten L5, L11, L17, L23 (auch DecoderLayer
                         :317, hookbar am Layer-Objekt).
 - modeling_argon.py:326-351  ArgonDecoderLayer.forward:
                         h_mid = h_in + post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)
                         (:345; attn auf input_layernorm(h_in) :336);
                         h_out = h_mid + post_feedforward_layernorm(moe_out)
                         (:350; MoE-Eingang pre_feedforward_layernorm :348).
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:239  Kanon-Pct-Konvention:
                         ungewichtete Assignment-Bincounts über top_indices,
                         normalisiert counts/counts.sum().clamp(min=1.0)*100.
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:292  Kanon-TV: 0.5 * (a-b).abs().sum().
 - train_moe_router_specialization.py:341  Trainings-Hook-Bereich
                         4 <= idx <= 23 (Begründung der Bänder).
 - machine_base.py:224-235  _encode/_gate_hooks-Muster (Vorgänger).

Prinzip (MechanisticCircuitMixMind v1.0, EINHEIT T1): Mittelwert ist kein
Beweis — diese API liefert FELDER (26x8-Matrizen, 26-Vektoren,
Layer-Trajektorien), nie Aggregate ohne Label. Pct-Zeilen sind
Fraction-Konvention (Summe 1.0 je Schicht); Prozent = x100.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import contextlib
from typing import Any, Dict, Iterator, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import torch


def canon_tv(a: torch.Tensor, b: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    """Kanon-TV auf Fraction-Skala (train_moe_router_specialization:292):
    0.5 * sum(|a-b|), Wertebereich [0,1]."""
    return 0.5 * float((a - b).abs().sum().item())


def ent_mean(t: torch.Tensor) -> float:
    """Mittlere Router-Entropie eines Schicht-Slices (float-Ausgabe)."""
    return float(t.detach().float().mean().item()) if t.numel() else 0.0


def layer_bands(num_layers: int) -> Tuple[List[int], List[int]]:
    """Registrierte Bänder der Tiefen-Hypothese: Edge {0..3, L-2, L-1},
    Core {4..L-3} — Training-Hook-Bereich 4 <= idx <= 23
    (train_moe_router_specialization.py:341)."""
    edge = [0, 1, 2, 3, num_layers - 2, num_layers - 1]
    core = list(range(4, num_layers - 2))
    return edge, core


class MechInterpProbe:
    """Rein-lesende Messeinheit: layerwise Routing-Felder + Logit-Lens."""

    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer, device: Optional[torch.device] = None,
                 max_length: int = 128):
        self.model = model
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.device = device if device is not None else next(model.parameters()).device
        self.max_length = int(max_length)
        self.n_layers = len(model.model.layers)
        self.n_experts = int(getattr(model.config, "n_routed_experts", 8))
        self.model.eval()

    # -- Encoding ----------------------------------------------------------
    def _encode(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.tokenizer(
            text, return_tensors="pt", truncation=True,
            max_length=self.max_length,
        )["input_ids"].to(self.device)

    # -- Reinheits-Fingerprint (EINHEIT S2) --------------------------------
    def fingerprint(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """expert_bias + gate.weight je Schicht — torch.equal-vergleichbar."""
        return {
            "expert_bias": [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.cpu().clone()
                            for layer in self.model.model.layers],
            "gate_weight": [layer.moe.gate.weight.data.cpu().clone()
                            for layer in self.model.model.layers],
        }

    def fingerprint_equal(self, a: Dict[str, Any], b: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
        ok = all(torch.equal(x, y) for x, y in
                 zip(a["expert_bias"], b["expert_bias"]))
        return ok and all(torch.equal(x, y) for x, y in
                          zip(a["gate_weight"], b["gate_weight"]))

    # -- MI-1: layerwise Routing-Tomographie -------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def layerwise_routing(self, texts: Sequence[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """26x8 Assignment-Counts (Kanon-Konvention, ungewichtet) +
        mittlere Router-Entropie je Schicht über alle Tokens aller Texte."""
        L, E = self.n_layers, self.n_experts
        counts = torch.zeros((L, E), dtype=torch.float64)
        ent_sum = torch.zeros(L, dtype=torch.float64)
        ent_tok = torch.zeros(L, dtype=torch.float64)

        def _hook(layer_idx: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                top_indices, _, router_ent = out
                counts[layer_idx] += torch.bincount(
                    top_indices.reshape(-1), minlength=E).double().cpu()
                ent_sum[layer_idx] += ent_mean(router_ent) * float(router_ent.numel())
                ent_tok[layer_idx] += float(router_ent.numel())
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].moe.gate.register_forward_hook(
            _hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        try:
            for text in texts:
                ids = self._encode(text)
                _ = self.model(ids)
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()

        row_sums = counts.sum(dim=1, keepdim=True).clamp(min=1.0)
        pct_frac = (counts / row_sums).float()
        ent = (ent_sum / ent_tok.clamp(min=1.0)).float()
        return {
            "counts_26x8": counts.float(),
            "pct_26x8": pct_frac,           # Zeilen summen 1.0 (Kanon: x100 fuer %)
            "pct_percent_26x8": pct_frac * 100.0,
            "entropy_26": ent,
            "n_tokens": int(ent_tok.sum().item()),
        }

    # -- MI-1: Logit-Lens über alle Schichten ------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def layerwise_logits(
        self, text: str, answer_targets: Optional[Dict[str, Sequence[int]]] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Residualstrom-Lens: für jedes h in [Emb, Layer0..Layer{n-1}] der
        offizielle Kopf-Pfad norm -> lm_head (Softcap des Checkpoints
        beachten; argon_1b_machines: None) -> P; Top-1-Trajektorie +
        Antwort-Token-Wahrscheinlichkeit + Gap-TV gegen die fp32-Referenz
        der letzten Schicht. Official-bf16-Logits werden als Anker
        mitgeloggt (anchor_tv, fp32-Lens-vs-bf16-Kopf-Kanal)."""
        L = self.n_layers
        captured: List[torch.Tensor] = []

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: captured.append(_out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        emb_handle = self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: captured.insert(0, o.detach()))
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
            emb_handle.remove()

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm
        head = self.model.lm_head

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Exakt der offizielle Kopf-Pfad (modeling_argon.py:615/:733,
            # bf16-Kette), Softmax über die fp32-Streuwerte der logits:
            # keine dtype-Neuerfindung; anchor_tv deckt die Parität.
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        p_final = _lens(captured[-1])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        official_top1 = int(torch.argmax(official_p).item())

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for idx, h in enumerate(captured):
            p = _lens(h)
            top1 = int(torch.argmax(p).item())
            row: Dict[str, Any] = {
                "layer": -1 if idx == 0 else idx - 1,
                "kind": "emb" if idx == 0 else "layer",
                "top1_id": top1,
                "top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode([top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_prob": float(p[top1].item()),
                "gap_tv": canon_tv(p, p_final),
            }
            if answer_targets:
                traj: Dict[str, float] = {}
                for label, cands in answer_targets.items():
                    vals = [float(p[t].item()) for t in cands if 0 <= t < p.numel()]
                    traj[label] = max(vals) if vals else 0.0
                row["answer_prob"] = traj
            rows.append(row)

        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "p_final_top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                [int(torch.argmax(p_final).item())], skip_special_tokens=True),
            "official_top1_id": official_top1,
            "official_top1_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                [official_top1], skip_special_tokens=True),
            "official_top1_prob": float(official_p[official_top1].item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }

    # -- MI-2: Sublayer-Zerlegung (Attention vs MoE) ------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def sublayer_logits(self, text: str, answer_target: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Halbschichten-Lens nach modeling_argon.py:326-351:

        h_mid(l) = h_out(l-1) + post_attention_layernorm(attn_out)   (:345)
        h_out(l) = h_mid(l) + post_feedforward_layernorm(moe_out)    (:350)

        Capture exakt: forward-hook auf layer.post_attention_layernorm
        (Output = norm(attn_out), bf16) + Layer-Output-Hook (h_out, MI-1-
        Muster) + Emb-Hook (h_out(-1)). h_mid ist die ORIGINAL-bf16-Addition
        :345 — keine Re-Normierung, keine dtype-Neuerfindung (EINHEIT S3).
        P je sublayer über den offiziellen Kopf-Pfad (_lens: norm -> lm_head,
        softmax fp32). dp_attn/dp_moe auf der registrierten target-Koordinate;
        tv_in_mid = canon_tv(P_in, P_mid) als Architektur-Profil-Feld.
        """
        L = self.n_layers
        h_out_all: List[torch.Tensor] = []      # h_out(l) fuer l = -1..L-1
        normed_attn: List[Optional[torch.Tensor]] = [None] * L

        def _norm_hook(li: int):
            def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
                normed_attn[li] = out.detach()
            return hook_fn

        handles = [self.model.model.layers[idx].register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, _out_layer(o)))
            for idx in range(L)]
        emb_handle = self.model.model.embed_tokens.register_forward_hook(
            lambda _m, _i, o: _store_layer(h_out_all, o.detach(), first=True))
        handles += [self.model.model.layers[idx].post_attention_layernorm
                    .register_forward_hook(_norm_hook(idx)) for idx in range(L)]
        official_logits = None
        ids = None
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            outputs = self.model(ids)
            official_logits = outputs.logits[0, -1].detach()
        finally:
            for handle in handles:
                handle.remove()
            emb_handle.remove()

        if any(v is None for v in normed_attn):
            raise RuntimeError(
                "sublayer_logits: post_attention_layernorm-Hook fehlte "
                f"({sum(v is None for v in normed_attn)} Schichten) — "
                "Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")
        if len(h_out_all) != L + 1:
            raise RuntimeError(
                f"sublayer_logits: {len(h_out_all)} statt {L + 1} h_out-"
                "Captures — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback.")

        norm_mod = self.model.model.norm   # ArgonModel-Submodul (Canon :362;
        head = self.model.lm_head          # :626-634 trägt nur lm_head)

        def _lens(h: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
            # Exakt der offizielle Kopf-Pfad (modeling_argon.py:615/:733,
            # bf16-Kette), Softmax über die fp32-Streuwerte der logits:
            logits = head(norm_mod(h[0, -1]))
            return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)

        target_id = int(answer_target["id"])
        official_p = torch.softmax(official_logits.float(), dim=-1)
        p_final = _lens(h_out_all[-1])
        p_emb = _lens(h_out_all[0])

        rows: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for li in range(L):
            h_mid = h_out_all[li] + normed_attn[li]     # exakt :345 (bf16)
            p_in = _lens(h_out_all[li])                 # P(h_out(l-1))
            p_mid = _lens(h_mid)                        # P(h_mid(l))
            p_out = _lens(h_out_all[li + 1])            # P(h_out(l))
            top1_out_id = int(torch.argmax(p_out).item())
            layer = self.model.model.layers[li]
            rows.append({
                "layer": li,
                "is_global": bool(layer.is_global),
                "p_in": float(p_in[target_id].item()),
                "p_mid": float(p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "p_out": float(p_out[target_id].item()),
                "dp_attn": float(p_mid[target_id].item() - p_in[target_id].item()),
                "dp_moe": float(p_out[target_id].item() - p_mid[target_id].item()),
                "tv_in_mid": canon_tv(p_in, p_mid),
                # Registered gap-Lesart (MI-1-Definition, canon_tv): Lens-TV
                # der Schicht-p_out-Verteilung gegen die finale Verteilung
                # p_final (h_out(25)) — Roh-Feld für die Tail-Kontraktion.
                "gap_out_tv": canon_tv(p_out, p_final),
                "top1_out_id": top1_out_id,
                "top1_out_str": self.tokenizer.decode(
                    [top1_out_id], skip_special_tokens=True),
                "top1_out_prob": float(p_out[top1_out_id].item()),
            })

        telescope = sum(r["dp_attn"] + r["dp_moe"] for r in rows)
        return {
            "rows": rows,
            "target_id": target_id,
            "target_str": answer_target.get("str", ""),
            "p_emb": float(p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "p_final": float(p_final[target_id].item()),
            "p_final_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(p_final).item()),
            "official_top1_id": int(torch.argmax(official_p).item()),
            "anchor_tv": canon_tv(p_final, official_p),
            "telescope_target": telescope,
            "endpoints_target": float(p_final[target_id].item() - p_emb[target_id].item()),
            "telescope_broken": bool(abs(telescope - (p_final[target_id].item()
                                                      - p_emb[target_id].item())) > 1e-6),
            "n_tokens": int(ids.shape[1]),
        }

    # -- Kristallisations-Finder (EINHEIT T2: Trajektorie statt Mittelwert) --
    def crystallization_start(self, rows: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]]) -> Optional[int]:
        """Kleinsten stabilen Layer-Start lesen (Modulfunktion)."""
        return crystallization_start(rows)

    # -- MI-3: Kausale Interventionen (EINHEIT C1–C3) ----------------------
    def _buffers(self, layers: Optional[Sequence[int]]) -> List[int]:
        sel = list(range(self.n_layers)) if layers is None \
            else [int(l) for l in layers]
        for l in sel:
            if not (0 <= l < self.n_layers):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Intervention: Schicht {l} außerhalb [0, {self.n_layers})")
        return sorted(set(sel))

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def steer_band(self, experts: Sequence[int],
                   layers: Optional[Sequence[int]] = None,
                   steer: float = 50.0) -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Hinreichendheits-Lesart (EINHEIT C1): expert_bias NUR in den
        gegebenen Schichten auf das Template gesetzt (+steer für experts,
        -steer für den Rest) — SET-Semantik nach machine_base.py:249-256
        (dict-Arm), band-limitiert. Restaurierung exakt (C3)."""
        experts = [int(e) for e in experts]
        if not experts:
            raise ValueError("steer_band: experts leer — kein Band-Steering.")
        if not steer > 0:
            raise ValueError("steer_band: steer muss > 0 sein (Amplitude).")
        sel = self._buffers(layers)
        layers_all = self.model.model.layers
        original = [layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone()
                    for l in sel]
        try:
            for l in sel:
                apply_band_steer(layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data,
                                 experts, steer)
            yield {"experts": experts, "layers": sel, "steer": float(steer),
                   "mode": "set"}
        finally:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for l, orig in zip(sel, original):
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.copy_(orig)
            bad = [l for l, orig in zip(sel, original)
                   if not torch.equal(
                       layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, orig)]
            if bad:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"steer_band: Restaurierung nicht bitidentisch in "
                    f"Schichten {bad} — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback "
                    "(EINHEIT C3).")

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def knockout_experts(self, experts: Sequence[int],
                         layers: Optional[Sequence[int]] = None,
                         penalty: float = -50.0) -> Iterator[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Notwendigkeits-Lesart (EINHEIT C1): die gegebenen Experten in den
        gegebenen Schichten ADDITIV stummschalten (g[e] += penalty auf dem
        GELERNTEN Original-Bias), die übrigen Experten tragen lernende
        Bias weiter (MI-3-Audits). Restaurierung exakt (C3)."""
        experts = [int(e) for e in experts]
        if not experts:
            raise ValueError("knockout_experts: experts leer.")
        if penalty >= 0:
            raise ValueError("knockout_experts: penalty muss < 0 sein "
                             "(Stummschaltung).")
        sel = self._buffers(layers)
        layers_all = self.model.model.layers
        original = [layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone()
                    for l in sel]
        try:
            for l in sel:
                apply_expert_knockout(
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, experts, penalty)
            yield {"experts": experts, "layers": sel, "penalty": float(penalty),
                   "mode": "additive"}
        finally:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for l, orig in zip(sel, original):
                    layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data.copy_(orig)
            bad = [l for l, orig in zip(sel, original)
                   if not torch.equal(
                       layers_all[l].moe.gate.expert_bias.data, orig)]
            if bad:
                raise RuntimeError(
                    f"knockout_experts: Restaurierung nicht bitidentisch in "
                    f"Schichten {bad} — Gerät-Bruch, kein stiller Fallback "
                    "(EINHEIT C3).")

    @torch.no_grad()
    def digit_mass(self, text: str, tau: float = 0.01) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """M_digit = Σ_d P_full(id(d)) über die voll-Vokabular-Softmax
        (SP3-Folge-Canon, machine_base.py:281-322). Resolution wie der
        Canon: je Ziffer ein einziges Token mit decode.lstrip == str(d);
        sonst ValueError (kein stiller Fallback). Meldung 'unknown' als
        Feld, nicht als Rückgabe-Abkürzung (M_raw < tau -> unknown=True)."""
        p = self.last_probs(text)
        digit_ids: List[int] = []
        for d in range(10):
            ids = self.tokenizer.encode(str(d), add_special_tokens=False)
            token_id = int(ids[0]) if len(ids) == 1 else -1
            decoded = (self.tokenizer.decode([token_id], skip_special_tokens=True)
                       if token_id >= 0 else "?")
            if token_id < 0 or decoded.lstrip(" ") != str(d):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"digit_mass: Ziffer '{d}' löst nicht als ein-Ziffern-"
                    f"Token auf (decode='{decoded}') — kein stiller Fallback.")
            digit_ids.append(token_id)
        probs = p.index_select(
            0, torch.tensor(digit_ids, device=p.device))
        mass = float(probs.sum().item())
        top_d = int(torch.argmax(probs).item()) if mass > 0.0 else None
        return {"M_digit": mass, "unknown": mass < tau,
                "digit_probs": {str(d): float(probs[i].item())
                                for i, d in enumerate(range(10))},
                "top_digit": top_d}

    @torch.no_grad()
    def last_probs(self, text: str) -> torch.Tensor:
        """Offizielle End-Verteilung P(logits[last], softmax) — der Kopf-Pfad
        des Modells (Canon :615/:733), fp32-Softmax."""
        ids = self._encode(text)
        logits = self.model(ids).logits[0, -1].detach()
        return torch.softmax(logits.float(), dim=-1)


CRYSTAL_MIN_TAIL = 2  # Registrierung: stabiler TAIL >= 2 Schichten gilt als Fenster


def crystallization_start(rows: Sequence[Dict[str, Any]],
                          min_tail: int = CRYSTAL_MIN_TAIL) -> Optional[int]:
    """Kleinster Layer-Index, ab dem top1 stabil bis zum ENDE bleibt und der
    stabile TAIL mindestens ``min_tail`` Schichten spannt; None, wenn kein
    stabiler Start existiert (Oszillation, Via-Negativa-Datum)."""
    layer_rows = [r for r in rows if r["kind"] == "layer"]
    if not layer_rows:
        return None
    final_top1 = layer_rows[-1]["top1_id"]
    for start_idx in range(len(layer_rows)):
        span = len(layer_rows) - start_idx
        if span < min_tail:
            break
        if all(r["top1_id"] == final_top1 for r in layer_rows[start_idx:]):
            return layer_rows[start_idx]["layer"]
    return None


def _out_layer(o: Any) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Decoder-Layer-Output (h, layer_entropy, present_kv) -> Residual h."""
    return o[0] if isinstance(o, tuple) else o.detach()


def apply_band_steer(buffer: torch.Tensor, experts: Sequence[int],
                     steer: float) -> None:
    """Band-Steering auf EINE Bias-Zeile: SET-Semantik nach dem registrierten
    dict-Arm von machine_base.steer (fill_(-steer) + g[e]=+steer) — das
    Template ueberschreibt den gelernten Bias fuer die Dauer des Blocks;
    Restaurierung macht der ContextManager (C3, copy_-Muster)."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        buffer.fill_(-float(steer))
        for e in experts:
            e = int(e)
            if not (0 <= e < buffer.numel()):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"apply_band_steer: Experte {e} außerhalb [0, {buffer.numel()})")
            buffer[e] = float(steer)


def apply_expert_knockout(buffer: torch.Tensor, experts: Sequence[int],
                          penalty: float) -> None:
    """Experten-Knockout auf EINE Bias-Zeile: ADDITIV auf den gelernten
    Original-Bias (die Spezialisten werden stummgeschaltet, alle übrigen
    tragen lernende Bias weiter — Notwendigkeits-Lesart des MI-3-Audits).
    In-place; Restaurierung macht der ContextManager."""
    with torch.no_grad():
        for e in experts:
            e = int(e)
            if not (0 <= e < buffer.numel()):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"apply_expert_knockout: Experte {e} außerhalb "
                    f"[0, {buffer.numel()})")
            buffer[e] += float(penalty)


def _store_layer(store: List[torch.Tensor], h: torch.Tensor,
                 first: bool = False) -> None:
    """Geordnete h_out-Capture-Einheit (EINHEIT S2): Emb-Hook schiebt an
    index 0 (h_out(-1)); Layer-Hooks feuern je Layer in Forward-Reihenfolge
    und haengen an — die Liste ist dann [-1, 0, 1, ..., L-1]."""
    if first:
        store.insert(0, h)
    else:
        store.append(h)

"""
machine_base.py — Götterdreiheit-Protokoll: gemeinsame Kontrakte aller Maschinen.

Alle drei Maschinen (Gödel, Turing, Wittgenstein) — und ihre Komposition —
trennen dasselbe Messkonzept: den Gödel-Gap als normierte Differenz zwischen
Selbstvorhersage und symbolischem Ist:

    apply_gap(pred, actual, state) = |pred - actual| / (|state| + 1e-6)

Canon-Belege:
  - uni_241_enhanced_gtw_machine.py:155-168   — ``prediction = tanh(W @ state)``;
    ``gap = ||pred - actual|| / (||state|| + 1e-6)``; Append in ``gap_history``.
  - uni_241_enhanced_gtw_machine.py:172-189   — Trend-Mischnung
    (recent = mean letzer 5 vs. older; 1.3 → RISING, 0.7 → FALLING, sonst
    STABLE; < 5 Einträge → UNKNOWN).
  - uni_238_active_goedel_gap.py:220-240      — Paradigmwechsel-Detektor
    (ratio recent/baseline > threshold).
  - cognito-construct.lisp:21/186-255         — Lisp-Struktur mit
    ``goedel-gap-history`` (push: erstes Element ist der neueste Gap);
    Predict-Self per Vektor-Add, Update 0.95/0.05.
  - compare_gemma3_vs_argon.py:249-260        — Steering-Muster:
    ``expert_bias.data.clone()`` vor Mutation, ``fill_(-steer)`` +
    ``+steer`` auf den gewählten Experten, ``copy_``-Restaurierung.

Der ArgonOracle ist die externe Selbstmodell-Erweiterung (Maschinen-Mode
``"oracle"``): statt (bzw. ergänzend zu) dem gelernten ``self_model`` predet
Argon das symbolische Ist aus dem antrainierten Verständnis; der Gap misst,
wie weit das Netz die Maschine aus sich heraus vorhersagt. Im Mode
``"in_loop"`` steuert die Router-Entropie die Maschinendynamik live
(Tape-Budget, Loop→Expansion). Der Mode ist per MachineConfig wählbar —
beide Haken sitzen in den Maschinen, nicht im Argon-Kern (modeling_argon.py
und moe_layers.py bleiben unangetastet).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Sequence, Tuple

import numpy as np
import torch

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Canon-Gap (uni_241:157) und Trend-Mischnung (uni_241:172)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
GAP_EPS = 1e-6


def apply_gap(predicted: Sequence[float], actual: Sequence[float],
              state: Sequence[float], eps: float = GAP_EPS) -> float:
    """Normierter Gödel-Gap uni_241:157: ||pred - actual|| / (||state|| + eps)."""
    p = np.asarray(predicted, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.asarray(actual, dtype=np.float64)
    s = np.asarray(state, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(np.linalg.norm(p - a) / (np.linalg.norm(s) + eps))


def _safe_mean(values: Sequence[float]) -> float:
    values = list(values)
    return float(np.mean(values)) if values else 0.0


def gap_trend(history: Sequence[float]) -> Tuple[str, float]:
    """Trend + Mittelwert nach uni_241:172-189 (Thresholds 1.3 / 0.7)."""
    history = list(history)
    if not history:
        return "UNKNOWN", 0.0
    current = history[-1]
    avg = _safe_mean(history[-10:])
    if len(history) < 5:
        return "UNKNOWN", avg
    recent = _safe_mean(history[-5:])
    older = _safe_mean(history[-10:-5]) if len(history) >= 10 else _safe_mean(history[:-5])
    if recent > older * 1.3:
        return "RISING", avg
    if recent < older * 0.7:
        return "FALLING", avg
    return "STABLE", avg


@dataclass
class GapRecord:
    """JSON-stabiler Gap-Zustand (uni_241 get_gap_status)."""

    current: float
    trend: str
    avg: float
    history: List[float]

    @classmethod
    def from_history(cls, history: Sequence[float]) -> "GapRecord":
        history = list(history)
        trend, avg = gap_trend(history)
        return cls(current=history[-1] if history else 0.0, trend=trend, avg=avg, history=history)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Maschinen-Konfiguration / Schritt-Ergebnis (JSON-stabil)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
@dataclass
class MachineConfig:
    """Konfiguration aller Maschinen; mode wählt Oracle- vs. In-Loop-Haken."""

    seed: int = 42
    mode: str = "oracle"                 # "oracle" | "in_loop"
    dimensions: int = 8                  # uni_241: state/self_model-Dimension
    pool: str = "corrected"              # uni_205: "corrected" = {0, 1, 2, pi, e} (kein phi)
    steer: float = 50.0                  # expert_bias-Amplitude (compare-Suite: 50.0)
    max_steps: int = 256
    params: Dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)

    def with_params(self, **overrides: Any) -> "MachineConfig":
        merged = dict(self.params)
        merged.update(overrides)
        return MachineConfig(
            seed=self.seed, mode=self.mode, dimensions=self.dimensions,
            pool=self.pool, steer=self.steer, max_steps=self.max_steps, params=merged,
        )


@dataclass
class StepResult:
    """Ergebnis eines Maschinenschritts — vollständig JSON-serialisierbar."""

    machine: str
    step: int
    gap: float
    trend: str
    trace: List[str]
    invariants: Dict[str, Any]
    argon_meta: Dict[str, Any]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Lisp-Paritäts-RNG (utils.lisp:44-60) — exakt: LCG + Box-Muller
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class LCGRandom:
    """
    Bitgenaue Wiederholung des Lisp-eigenen deterministischen Stroms
    (utils.lisp:44-60) für P1-Parität Seed-basierter Lisp-Ops (ast_eval etc.):

        *rng-state* 123456789; set-rng-seed -> logand seed #xFFFFFFFF
        deterministic-random(limit):
            state = mod(a*state + c, 2^32)          a=1664525, c=1013904223
            float:  (* limit (/ (float state 1.0d0) m))
            int:    (mod state limit)
        random-normal: Box-Muller aus ZWEI float(1.0d0)-Zügen, u1 < 1e-10 -> 1e-10
    """

    A = 1664525
    C = 1013904223
    M = 4294967296  # 2^32
    DEFAULT_STATE = 123456789
    _U1_CLAMP = 1e-10

    def __init__(self, seed: Optional[int] = None):
        self.state = self.DEFAULT_STATE if seed is None else (seed & 0xFFFFFFFF)

    def reset(self, seed: Optional[int] = None) -> None:
        """set-rng-seed-Äquivalent (logand seed #xFFFFFFFF)."""
        self.state = self.DEFAULT_STATE if seed is None else (seed & 0xFFFFFFFF)

    def draw(self) -> int:
        self.state = (self.A * self.state + self.C) % self.M
        return self.state

    def integer(self, limit: int) -> int:
        """(mod *rng-state* limit) — limit muss int >= 1 sein."""
        return self.draw() % int(limit)

    def float(self, limit: float = 1.0) -> float:
        """(* limit (/ (float state 1.0d0) m)) — Reihenfolge wie Lisp (Division zuerst)."""
        return float(limit) * (float(self.draw()) / float(self.M))

    def normal(self) -> float:
        """random-normal (Box-Muller): sqrt(-2 ln u1) * cos(2 pi u2)."""
        u1 = self.float(1.0)
        u2 = self.float(1.0)
        if u1 < self._U1_CLAMP:
            u1 = self._U1_CLAMP
        return math.sqrt(-2.0 * math.log(u1)) * math.cos(2.0 * math.pi * u2)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ArgonOracle: externe Selbstmodell-Erweiterung (Mode "oracle")
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Expertenwahl-Doppelanker: Startvorschlag des Plans; machine_registry.json
# (Phase-A-Baseline, Schritt 5) überschreibt ihn, sobald er gemessen ist.
MACHINE_EXPERT_DEFAULTS: Dict[str, List[int]] = {
    "goedel": [2, 7],
    "turing": [0, 6],
    "wittgenstein": [1, 3],
}

N_ROUTED_EXPERTS_DEFAULT = 8


class ArgonOracle:
    """
    Liest aus Argon, was kein Symbol-Prozessors leisten kann: Zahlvorhersage
    aus den Logits, Router-Entropie der Text, verstecke Repräsentation — und
    lenkt das Routing über additiven ``expert_bias`` ohne Gewichtsupdate
    (Suite-Muster compare_gemma3_vs_argon.py:249-260, restore via ``copy_``).

    Nur eval + greedy + ``no_grad``; die Maschinen selbst sind deterministisch.
    """

    def __init__(self, model, tokenizer, device: Optional[torch.device] = None,
                 steer: float = 50.0, n_experts: int = N_ROUTED_EXPERTS_DEFAULT):
        self.model = model
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.device = device if device is not None else next(model.parameters()).device
        self.steer_amplitude = float(steer)  # Amplitude; Methode steer() heißt
        # unangetastet steer() — Attribut-Assign würde die Methode maskieren.
        self.n_experts = int(n_experts)
        self.model.eval()

    # -- Intern: Encoding + Gate-Entropie-Hooks (Muster: train_*:154-163) ----
    def _encode(self, text: str, max_length: int = 256) -> torch.Tensor:
        return self.tokenizer(text, return_tensors="pt", truncation=True,
                              max_length=max_length)["input_ids"].to(self.device)

    def _gate_hooks(self, collector: Dict[str, List[torch.Tensor]]):
        def hook_fn(_module, _inp, out):
            top_indices, _, router_ent = out
            collector["counts"] = collector["counts"] + torch.bincount(
                top_indices.reshape(-1), minlength=self.n_experts).float()
            collector["entropies"].append(router_ent.float().mean())
        return [layer.moe.gate.register_forward_hook(hook_fn)
                for layer in self.model.model.layers]

    # -- Öffentliche Lesekanäle ---------------------------------------------
    @torch.no_grad()
    def predict_value(self, prompt: str, max_digits: int = 6) -> Any:
        """
        Greedy Ziffern-Lesung aus der Vorhersageverteilung: der Argmax-Next-Token
        muss ein (einstelliges) Ziffern-Token sein ("0".." 9"); sonst
        ``"unknown"`` — kein stiller Fallback (Plan-Risiko 5).
        """
        import re
        if self.model.training:
            self.model.eval()
        ids = self._encode(prompt)
        digits = ""
        cache = None
        for _ in range(max_digits):
            outputs = self.model(input_ids=ids, past_key_values=cache, use_cache=True)
            logits = outputs.logits[0, -1]
            next_id = int(torch.argmax(logits).item())
            token_str = self.tokenizer.decode([next_id], skip_special_tokens=True)
            token_str = token_str.lstrip(" ")
            if not (len(token_str) == 1 and token_str.isdigit()):
                if not digits:
                    return "unknown"
                break
            digits += token_str
            cache = outputs.past_key_values
            ids = torch.tensor([[next_id]], device=ids.device)
        if not digits:
            return "unknown"
        # Konsistenz-Schicht: die Vorhersage muss eine Zahl sein.
        if re.fullmatch(r"-?\d+", digits) is None:
            return "unknown"
        return int(digits)

    @torch.no_grad()
    def predict_scale_0_9(self, prompt: str, tau: float = 0.01,
                          mode: str = "expected") -> Any:
        """
        S1 — Ein-Forward-Ziffern-Skala (SP3-Folge: das 6-Token-Greedy-Echo
        "100000" = "1" + "00000" ist autoregressives Nachhallen, kein Urteil
        über Gültigkeit). Die Logits des ERSTEN Tokens werden ausschließlich
        auf die 10 Ziffern-Token-IDs '0'..'9' projiziert; die Vollvokabular-
        Softmax-Masse

            M_digit = sum_d P_full(id(d))

        entscheidet ehrlich über den Stummkanal: M_digit < tau -> "unknown"
        (kein stiller Fallback), sonst eine kontinuierlich abgestufte
        Bewertung in [0,1] ohne Clip-Trick min(value/10, 1.0):

            mode="expected":  v = sum_d d * p_d / 9     (Erwartungswert)
            mode="argmax":    v = argmax_d p_d / 9
        """
        if self.model.training:
            self.model.eval()
        ids = self._encode(prompt)
        logits = self.model(ids).logits[0, -1].float()
        probs = torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)
        digit_ids: List[int] = []
        for digit in range(10):
            token_ids = self.tokenizer.encode(str(digit), add_special_tokens=False)
            token_id = int(token_ids[0]) if len(token_ids) else -1
            decoded = (self.tokenizer.decode([token_id], skip_special_tokens=True)
                       if token_id >= 0 else "?")
            if decoded.lstrip(" ") != str(digit):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"predict_scale_0_9: Ziffer '{digit}' ist kein "
                    f"einstelliges Token (decode='{decoded}') — Skala nicht "
                    "definierbar (kein stiller Fallback).")
            digit_ids.append(token_id)
        digit_probs = probs.index_select(
            0, torch.tensor(digit_ids, device=probs.device))
        if float(digit_probs.sum().item()) < tau:
            return "unknown"
        if mode == "argmax":
            return int(torch.argmax(digit_probs).item()) / 9.0
        weighted = (digit_probs * torch.arange(
            10, dtype=digit_probs.dtype, device=digit_probs.device)).sum()
        return float(weighted.item()) / 9.0

    @torch.no_grad()
    def entropy_scalar(self, text: str, max_length: int = 256) -> float:
        """Mittlere Router-Entropie über alle Schichten (Muster _gate_hooks)."""
        collector: Dict[str, List[torch.Tensor]] = {
            "counts": torch.zeros(self.n_experts, device=self.device),
            "entropies": [],
        }
        ids = self._encode(text, max_length)
        handles = self._gate_hooks(collector)
        try:
            _ = self.model(ids)
        finally:
            for h in handles:
                h.remove()
        if not collector["entropies"]:
            return 0.0
        return float(torch.stack(collector["entropies"]).mean().item())

    @torch.no_grad()
    def hidden_state(self, text: str, dimensions: Optional[int] = None) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Finaler Hidden-State (Hook an ``model.model.norm``, modeling_argon.py:615),
        Sequenz gemittelt und deterministisch auf ``dimensions`` (Default: die
        Maschinen-Dimension, 8) zusammengefallen: gemitteltes (hidden,) wird als
        (hidden/8, 8)-Streifen gemittelt — dokumentierte Reduktion, kein
        Canon-Anspruch.
        """
        d = int(dimensions) if dimensions else 8
        captured: List[torch.Tensor] = []

        def hook(_module, _inp, out):
            captured.append(out.detach())

        handle = self.model.model.norm.register_forward_hook(hook)
        try:
            ids = self._encode(text)
            _ = self.model(ids)
        finally:
            handle.remove()

        if not captured:
            raise RuntimeError("ArgonOracle.hidden_state: norm-Hook feuerte nicht.")
        pooled = captured[0].float().mean(dim=1).reshape(-1)  # (hidden,)
        if pooled.numel() % d != 0:
            raise ValueError(
                f"hidden_state: hidden={pooled.numel()} teilt nicht auf {d}.")
        return pooled.reshape(-1, d).mean(axis=0).cpu().numpy().astype(np.float64)

    # -- Steering: Preset-/Default-Anbindung (Suite-Muster) ------------------
    def _preset_experts(self, name_or_preset: Any) -> Tuple[List[int], float, Optional[List[float]], str]:
        """
        Löst einen Preset auf: dict direkt, Preset-Datei
        ``argon_1b_machines/machine_presets/<name>.json`` (falls vorhanden),
        sonst die Default-Expertenwahl; Registry (machine_registry.json)
        schlägt den Plan-Vorschlag, sobald er gemessen ist.
        """
        name = name_or_preset if isinstance(name_or_preset, str) else "combined"
        preset: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        if isinstance(name_or_preset, dict):
            preset = dict(name_or_preset)
        else:
            preset = load_preset(name) or load_registry_machine(name)

        experts = preset.get("experts") or MACHINE_EXPERT_DEFAULTS.get(
            name, list(range(self.n_experts)))
        experts = sorted(int(e) for e in experts if 0 <= int(e) < self.n_experts)
        steer = float(preset.get("steer", self.steer_amplitude))
        raw_bias = preset.get("expert_bias_values" if "expert_bias_values" in preset
                              else "expert_bias") if isinstance(preset, dict) else None
        if isinstance(raw_bias, dict):
            raw_bias = raw_bias.get("values")   # Preset-Schema: {layers, values}
        stored_bias = raw_bias if isinstance(raw_bias, (list, tuple)) else None
        source = ("preset-file" if preset else ("registry" if name != "combined" else "defaults"))
        return [int(e) for e in experts], steer, stored_bias, source

    @contextmanager
    def steer(self, name_or_preset: Any = "combined"):
        """
        Additives Routing-Steering ohne Gewichtsupdate: auf den gewählten
        Experten ``+steer``, auf alle übrigen ``-steer`` (fill_(-steer)-
        Muster); bei Preset mit gespeichertem ``expert_bias`` direkt
        ``copy_``. Die Original-Bias-Vektoren werden exakt restauriert, denn
        spezialisierte Checkpoints persistieren einen kalibrierten Bias.
        """
        experts, steer, stored_bias, source = self._preset_experts(name_or_preset)
        if not self.model.training:
            pass  # eval: Inline-Balancing bleibt inaktiv; kein Zustands-Toggle nötig
        orig_bias = [layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.clone()
                     for layer in self.model.model.layers]
        try:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for i, layer in enumerate(self.model.model.layers):
                    g = layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data
                    if stored_bias is not None:
                        start = i * self.n_experts           # flat n_layers·n_experts
                        values = torch.as_tensor(
                            stored_bias[start:start + self.n_experts],
                            dtype=g.dtype, device=g.device)
                        g.copy_(values)
                        continue
                    g.fill_(-steer)
                    for e in experts:
                        g[e] = steer
            yield {"experts": experts, "steer": steer, "stored_bias": stored_bias is not None,
                   "source": source}
        finally:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for layer, g0 in zip(self.model.model.layers, orig_bias):
                    layer.moe.gate.expert_bias.data.copy_(g0)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Registry-/Preset-Lader (Dateien werden in Schritt 4/5 geschrieben)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
_PRESET_DIRS = (
    "argon_1b_machines/machine_presets",
    "machines/presets",
)


def load_preset(name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Liest ``machine_presets/<name>.json`` aus einem der Preset-Verzeichnisse."""
    import os
    for directory in _PRESET_DIRS:
        path = os.path.join(directory, f"{name}.json")
        if os.path.isfile(path):
            with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
                return json.load(handle)
    return None


def load_registry_machine(name: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Liest eine Maschinen-Domäne aus machine_registry.json (falls vorhanden)."""
    import os
    path = "machine_registry.json"
    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        return None
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as handle:
        registry = json.load(handle)
    entry = (registry.get("machines") or {}).get(name)
    if not isinstance(entry, dict):
        return None
    cleaned = {k: v for k, v in entry.items() if k in ("experts", "steer", "entropy_target")}
    return cleaned or None


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basisklasse aller Maschinen (Plan: API-Symmetrie)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
class BaseMachine(ABC):
    """
    Gemeinsames Protokoll: reset / step / observe / state / gap / digest /
    describe. Die Maschinen halten ihre Canon-Mechanik selbst
    (uni_204/205/238/241/256, Lisp-Canon); die Basisklasse liefert nur den
    JSON-stabilen Zustands-/Digest-/Gap-Rahmen und die Observations-Schicht
    (Snapshot -> step -> Restore).
    """

    MACHINE_NAME = "base"

    def __init__(self, config: MachineConfig, oracle: Optional[ArgonOracle] = None):
        self.config = config
        self.oracle = oracle
        self._rng = np.random.default_rng(config.seed)  # uni_*-Canon-RNG
        self._step_index = 0
        self._trace: List[str] = []
        self._gap_history: List[float] = []

    # -- Protokoll ------------------------------------------------------------
    @abstractmethod
    def reset(self, seed: Optional[int] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Resonanzlos neu aufsetzen (Seed-Reset; gibt den Initial-State)."""

    @abstractmethod
    def step(self, obj: Any) -> StepResult:
        """Ein Maschinenschritt; mutierend; liefert Gap + Trace."""

    @abstractmethod
    def state(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """JSON-stabiler Zustand (sortierte Keys im Digest)."""

    @abstractmethod
    def describe(self) -> str:
        """Menschliche Kurzbeschreibung der Maschinen-Lage."""

    def observe(self, obj: Any) -> StepResult:
        """Nicht-mutierende Beobachtung: Snapshot -> step -> Restore."""
        snapshot = self._snapshot()
        try:
            return self.step(obj)
        finally:
            self._restore_snapshot(snapshot)

    def _snapshot(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        raise NotImplementedError(
            f"{self.MACHINE_NAME}: observe() braucht _snapshot/_restore_snapshot.")

    def _restore_snapshot(self, snapshot: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        raise NotImplementedError(
            f"{self.MACHINE_NAME}: observe() braucht _snapshot/_restore_snapshot.")

    @property
    def mode(self) -> str:
        return self.config.mode

    @property
    def step_index(self) -> int:
        return self._step_index

    @property
    def trace(self) -> List[str]:
        return list(self._trace)

    # -- Gap-/Digest-Rahmen ---------------------------------------------------
    def note_gap(self, predicted: Sequence[float], actual: Sequence[float],
                 state: Sequence[float]) -> float:
        """Canon-Gap anwenden und in der History anhängen (Append = neuester)."""
        gap = apply_gap(predicted, actual, state)
        self._gap_history.append(gap)
        return gap

    def gap(self) -> GapRecord:
        return GapRecord.from_history(self._gap_history)

    def digest(self) -> str:
        payload = json.dumps(self.state(), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"),
                             ensure_ascii=False)
        return hashlib.sha256(payload.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

    # -- Argon-Haken ----------------------------------------------------------
    def _oracle_prediction(self, prompt: str, state: Sequence[float]) -> Any:
        """
        Canon-Verallgemeinerung (uni_241:157): Argon predet das symbolische Ist.
        Numerische Vorhersage (predict_value) oder None — Maschinen entscheiden
        selbst, ob ein fehlender Oracle-Prediction-Wert den Schritt abbricht
        (in_loop-Fallback) oder als Gap-„unknown" zählt.
        """
        if self.oracle is None or self.config.mode != "oracle":
            return None
        return self.oracle.predict_value(prompt)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Fabrik
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
def make_machine(name: str, config: Optional[MachineConfig] = None,
                 oracle: Optional[ArgonOracle] = None) -> BaseMachine:
    """Fabrik: "goedel" | "turing" | "wittgenstein" | "composite"."""
    if config is None:
        config = MachineConfig()
    if name == "goedel":
        from machines.goedel_machine import GoedelMachine
        return GoedelMachine(config, oracle=oracle)
    if name == "turing":
        from machines.turing_machine import TuringMachine
        return TuringMachine(config, oracle=oracle)
    if name == "wittgenstein":
        from machines.wittgenstein_machine import WittgensteinMachine
        return WittgensteinMachine(config, oracle=oracle)
    if name == "composite":
        from machines.composite import CompositeMachine
        return CompositeMachine(config, oracle=oracle)
    raise ValueError(f"make_machine: unbekannte Maschine {name!r} "
                     "(erlaubt: goedel, turing, wittgenstein, composite)")
